# Buddy Task and Markdown Taxonomy Systems

**Status:** `catalogued` subsystem specifications; not evidence of implementation.

This companion inventory creates one independent Buddy subsystem for every category in the human-AI software task sandbox and the Markdown implementation-gap policy. Similar labels from different taxonomies remain separate.

- **Systems:** 369
- **Groups:** 11
- **Machine-readable source:** `config/buddy/task-taxonomy-system-registry.json`
- **Published mirror:** `website/data/task-taxonomy-system-registry.json`
- **Dashboard:** `website/category-systems.html`

## Group totals

- **task_action:** 135
- **task_domain:** 80
- **user_context:** 25
- **task_complexity:** 8
- **task_modality:** 8
- **execution_environment:** 11
- **task_quality_dimension:** 24
- **business_client_scenario:** 37
- **personal_task_scenario:** 31
- **markdown_gap_status:** 5
- **markdown_reference_path_root:** 5

## Implementation gate

Every item must receive its own owner, interface, source mapping, tests, benchmark or checklist, regression evidence, and dashboard status before it can advance beyond `catalogued`. Shared profiles are reusable defaults; they do not merge category identity or verification.
